10a1(1): @#//gnas pa yi//khang pa mthon po rnams dang 'a bigs byad ri rtse 'gron pa'i bal glang dang //gdugs dang rnga yab bud med g.yog 'khor rin chen 
10a2(1): gser dang rta rnams dang //bzang mdzes de dag kun kyang sngon gyi sbyin pa'i dge ba'i rnam smin yin//pan du ka ltar rtsa dmar dgun chang gser snod bzang por legs  
10a3(1): blugs pa//yongs dag a mra'i smin ltar dri zhim ud pal 'gying zhing ldem//na chung zhen pa mna' sgog dus kyis stobs shing yams grangs mang 'grim pa//mchog tu dge 
10a4(1): ba de rgyu smin pa yin zhes blo gros dag pas gsungs//gser gyi phag skas rked bcings snyan smras 'dzum zhing smin ma ya cig (ba)skyod//mig yangs rnag thug dkar nag   
10a5(1): 'byes la sgra 'bab me tog phreng bkris pa'i//na chung bzang mo brlar rgyas nyid legs byas rnams kyi bran ltar byed cing //tshogs bsags 'bras de sbyin pa'i che ba nyid   
10a6(1): ces zas gtsang sras pos gsungs//de lta lags pas bde bar gshegs pa'i gsung rab mtha' dag thugs chud pa//shin tu rgyas pa'i chu ltar gyur pa snying po dri ma med 
10a7(1): pa dang //lha'i skye bo dang klu'i gnas su gyur pa//tshul khrims 'dren pa ma yin zhing rgya che ba'i chu dang ldan pa dge ba la rnam par rtog pa'i zla ba shar ba'i dbang gis//ting 
10a8(1): nge 'dzin sna tshogs rgyas pa'i dus kyi rlabs kyis byang chub kyi yan lag skyon med pa'i nor bu mang po kun tu g.yangs pa//sems kyi 'og gzhi la 'phags pa'i lam yan 
10b1(1): lag brgyad pa'i ri bo chen po brten pa/'bras bu'i bye brag thob pa/gang zag chen po'i chu sprin rgyu ba/'phags pa'i bden pa dang /yang dag par spong ba dang /rdzu 
10b2(1): 'phrul gyi rkang pa'i shing ljon pas mdzes par byas pa/dran pa nye bar bzhag pa'i dogs dang ldan pa/sgrib pa'i ro mi gnas pa/sbyangs pa'i yon tan goms pa'i dag pa'i 
10b3(1): mu tig gi nya phyis kyi khog lus dang ldan pa/mngon par rtogs pa'i chos nyid gting mi dpogs shing zab pa/rgyun mi 'chad par klog pa dang /kha ton byed pa'i sgra rgya cher   
10b4(1): 'byung ba/'phags pa'i dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi rgya mtsho chen po la bdag gsol ba 'tshal lo//'di lta ste srid pa'i 'gro ba gcig nas gcig tu rgyud pa du ma nas 
10b5(1): dge ba'i sa bon btab pa/bde bar gshegs pa'i bstan pa/gzhal med khang gis bzhi gcig par gyur pa/bzod pa dang /nges pa dang /byams pa dang /tshul 
10b6(1): khrims dang /thos pa dang /zhi ba'i gnas/bdag dang gzhan gyi gzhung don gyi de kho na ma lus par rtogs pa/yongs su shes pas bsgoms pas dran pa nye bar   
10b7(1): gnas pa/yang dag pa'i ye shes mthong bas log par lta ba'i rab rib bsal ba/legs par brjod cing shis la rgya che ba'i mi dang ldan pa'i skye bo bla ma la bsnyen  
10b8(1): bkur byed pa dang /mngon du smra ba dang /phyag 'tshal ba la sogs pa'i tshul bzang po dang ldan zhing mdong gsol che bas snyen pa rnams bskyed pa/ston gyi zla ba nya ba'i 
11a1(1): @#//'od zer dag pa lta bu'i yid 'byung ba/sbyin bdag chen po che ba'i bdag nyid can ming 'di zhes bya ba 'di ni/khyim bdag legs sbyin dang ;
11a2(1): bzang ldan dang /btsun pa 'khor gyi bla dang /mu shil dang /tra mu sa dang /go shil dang /sta ba ka ra ni sa dang /mgon med zas sbyin dang /drag po dang /nag po dang /
11a3(1): nam gru la sogs pa dang /gzhan yang bcom ldan 'das la yid mngon par dad pa/gsal rgyal dang /tshangs pas byin dang /rgyal po shar ba'i bu dang /gtum po  
11a4(1): rab snang la sogs pa sa'i steng la dbang ba'i sbyin pa las byung ba'i legs pa spyod pa'i rjes su slo ba ste/lus dang longs spyod dang /srog dang sgyu ma dang smig rgyu dang /dri 
11a5(1): za'i grong khyer dang /'gal me bskor ba dang /rmi lam dang /ri'i 'bab chu'i chu ltar g.yo bar rig nas/stobs bcu mnga' bas gsungs pas tshigs su bcad pa dang ;
11a6(1): chos mngon pa dang /'dul ba dang /mdo sde 'dzin cing /dge ba'i las kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa yod do//bsod nams kyi sa bon bskyed pa'i rgyu 'khor ba'i dge 
11a7(1): ba phun sum tshogs pa'i gzhi/skye bo ngan pas rnyed par dka' ba ser sna can gyi yid 'jom par bsgrub pa na/ston gyi zla ba dri ma med pa ltar/dang ba'i thugs mnga' ba/
11a8(1): srid pa'i nyon mongs pa'i sa bon gcod pa/sems can thams cad kyi bsam pa mkhyen pa/gragspa'i 'jig rten gsum du khyab pa/bdud kyi bu mo stag pa rgyun gcod  
11b1(1): pa'i rgyu/skyabs su 'os pa'i chos ston pa/'gro ba la dge ba'i bya dka' ba'i phrin las rnams kyis/chos thams cad thugs su chud pas rnam par sangs rgyas pa'o|
11b2(1): sangs rgyas nyi ma'i 'od zer gyi snang ba gsal bas reg nas pad+ma'i 'dab ma rgyas pa ltar/ring zhing rdzi ma legs ba'i spyan dang ldan pa/gnod sbyin gyi bdag po'i gtsug 
11b3(1): gi nor bu chen pos zhabs kyi sor mo kun tu bskor ba/gser gyi mchog sdong ltar gsal ba/sku la rdo rje dang lcags kyu dang /pad+ma dang /nya dang /rgyal mtshan dang |
11b4(1): gdugs dang /'khor lo la sogs pa'i mtshan rnams kyi mtshan mnga' ba/pad+ma'i 'byung gnas rnams la/snang mdzad kyi 'od kyi phreng ba bye brag med par khyab pa(sa)
11b5(1): rgyas par 'gengs pa/bung ba'i tshogs ltar sngo ba'i gtsug tor gyi nang nas kun 'byung ba'i 'od kyi dra bas phyogs kyi dkyil 'khor ma lus par bkra bar byas pas |
11b6(1): kha na ma tho ba med par zhugs pa/'phags pa'i bden pa ston pa/zhi bar gzhol ba'i lam du 'dren pa/'jig rten sgrol ba mnyes gshin pa//gnyen gcig pu |
11b7(1): mu stegs can ngan pa'i rgol ba rnams kyis gsung dang /thugs dang /dpa' ba dang /mthu la mi gnod pa gtsang ma'i sras yul rnams kun la sred pa 'byung ba rgyun   
11b8(1): skams byed pa'i rgyu/skye bo mkhas pa rnams ni sad par bya phyir shar gyur pa/bag la nyal gyi rab rib bsal ba ye shes snang ba'i mchog/sa rnams mtha' dag 
12a1(1): @#//snang ba phyogs rnams thams cad snang mdzad pa//phyag dang zhabs dang dbu ni rab tu byin zhing tshul khrims dang //smon lam dag gis byang chub lam bzang 
12a2(1): yang dag bsgrubs gyur pa//srid pa'i 'gro ba'i dug gis snye ma'i steng du lhung ba yi//skye dgu rnams la kha na ma tho med pa ston mdzad pa//srid pa rnam pa gsum du 'jig pa|
12a3(1): bcom ldan 'das stobs bcu'i stobs dang ldan pa/mi 'jigs pa bzhi mnga' ba/dpa' ba dang /mthu kha na ma mtho ba med pa dang /mtshan dang dpe byad bzang pos brgyan 
12a4(1): pas sku brjid pa/pha rol tu phyin pa drug dang /dka' ba brgya phrag du mas dam pa'i chos dkon mchog bsgrubs pa/bstsal ba tsam gyis sems can mtha' dag gis bsam 
12a5(1): pa thugs su chud pa/thams cad mkhyen pa'i 'phags pa nyan thos kyi dge 'dun rab tu zhi ba dang /tshul khrims dang /thos pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /bzod pa dang /byams pa  
12a6(1): sbyangs pa'i yon tan dang ldan pa/nyon mongs pa'i rab ri ba dang /ling tog ma lus par bsal ba/'khor ba'i rang bzhin rig pa dang /phyogs phyogs kyi gtsug lag khang dang /
12a7(1): kun dga' ra ba dang /ri dgas nags 'dab dang /khrad khrod dang /chu dang ri sul dang /gsher dang ne'u sing dang /shing gi gri ba ma rnam pa sna tshogs la dgyes pa yul dang /ljongs 
12a8(1): du ma la phan gdags par stsol bar mdzad pa/yang dag pa'i dran pa dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /dga' ba thob pa las ma nyams pa/phung po lnga spyir skad cig par yang dag par  
12b1(1): so sor rtogs pa'i yid la byed pa skyed pa'i mdo sde dang /'dul ba dang /chos mngon pa dang /skyes pa'i rabs dang /gleng gzhi dang /tshigs su bcad pa dang /rtogs pa 
12b2(1): brjod pa dang /de lta bu byung ba dang /gtan phab pa'i gtam gyi sems sbyangs pa/rang gi lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyi 'jug pa dran pa nye bar gnas pa rgyu ba dbang po dang  
12b3(1): stobs dang /byang chub kyi yan lag dang /bden pa dang /'phags pa'i lam dang /dran pa nye bar bzhag pa sgom pa goms pas dad pa thob pa/bu mo phrugs kyis nyams 
12b4(1): dga' la//ri sul mthon po dpag bsam nags mang dang //ri bgab rnams ni gang dag bzhugs pa dang //shing gi me tog 'brul bas dri zhim byas pa'i ser bu ngad 
12b5(1): ldang zhing //nags na glang po 'dreg pa zag pas bsgoms pa'i rin chen khri shing khyim//khyim la chags pa med pa'i lha yis sten pa'i bsod nams chu gling gi/gnya' shing 
12b6(1): 'dzin khri bya rgyags khyu gnas pa na gang dag bzhugs pa dang/mal mos brnyogs snod pa yi rtsal 'gyur tshal//me tog ldan zhing ser bu ngad ldang ba//sa h+ya ri bo ngos ni 
12b7(1): yangs pa la//yongs dag thugs mda' thub pa bzhugs dgu dang //pad+ma ud+pal rgyas pa 'byung ba'i gnas//nyin re nyi ma'i rta sa rdo snod can//srid pa'i 'jigs bral 
12b8(1): ra ba yid 'ong ba//'char ba'i ri steng gang dag bzhugs pa dang //ri'i rtse mos sngo sangs sprin thal sman ljong can//dogs ldan chu bo man+d+ha ras ri lus dkrus//glang po'i mche 
13a1(1): ci @#//bas brnyogs pa'i sa phyogs glogs pa'i ri//'bigs byed rma bya rgyags gnas pa na bzhugs dgu dang //sna tshogs 'byung kun tshon gyi kha bsgyur  
13a2(1): phug dang ldan zhing sprin dang chags gyur la//ko lpags ldan pa gtsang ma'i chu yi dogs kyi rdo ba mang po bkrus gyur cig/ngos la ba lang 'jug ma mang po 'phyang zhing glog   
13a3(1): 'gyus snang byas pa/yongs 'dus ri rtse phrag na gcig pu sred dang bral ba gang dag bzhugs pa dang /gang dag shing dang khri shing rnam pa mang po 'dab chags par gnas pa//ne'u 
13a4(1): sing 'jam la sngo ba'i steng na/rma bya'i khyu gar byed cig/sgra snyan pa 'byin pa/ri dgas kyi tshogs pag yangs su gnas pa'i sos bcad pa rtswa'i myu gu yod ba me tog gi 
13a5(1): dri zhim pos bsgos pa'i ser bu 'jam zhing bsil ba'i ngad ldang ba'i nags 'dab rnams su gnas bcas te/bzhugs pa rnams dang /yang gang dag dus su rlabs byung   
13a6(1): ba'i rba rla bas kyis g.yengs pa'i na le sham dang /li shi dang /la ba la'i 'bras bu sngon po mang po 'grigs pa'i mtshan dang /a ga ru'i shing khrod na gnas pa'i sbrul mgo 
13a7(1): gdengs shing 'jigs su rung ba yod pa/glang po dregs pa dang bcas pa'i gnas byi ru'i sdong po sgyel ba dang ldan zhing /nyi ma'i 'od zer gyis reg pa'i dbang gis mu tig 
13a8(1): yod pa'i nya phyis kha bye ba'i thog mas rnam par spras pa/nye bar 'dul ba dang ldan la/nor bu mchog rdo la grub pa'i chung ma'i skye bo gnas shing bya'i khyu dga' ste sgra 'byin pas 
13b1(1): glu len pa lta bu yod pa'i rgya mtsho'i gling dag na/klu'i bdag po'i lus chen pos sku dkris te/dengs ka'i gdugs kyis nyi ma'i 'od zer sgribs shing /bsam gtan 
13b2(1): mdzad pa rnams dang /sman ljongs dag na phyogs kyi glang chen brang gis rdo ba khyebs pa'i khyu 'khod cing /'khor bcas lhan cig ma yin rol pa'i gnod sbyin mo dag   
13b3(1): gnas gyur la/klu'i rgyal po mgo bo stod de/kha nas 'od 'bar byung bas shing sreg cing /gangs ri ti se la ni blo gtsang gang dag zhal thon mdzad cing bzhugs pa dang /rgya 
13b4(1): mtsho rlabs kyis bshal ba'i ri bag ba ldan pa ma smad pa//ri bo man+d+ha ra la nyin phyed tshun chad bzhugs bzhugs shing //nyi ma phyed par gyur nas gang dag gzhan du bzhugs 
13b5(1): pa'i phyir//nor bus rtse mo bkram pa 'khor yug ri la sten pa dang //gang dag khri shing gzhon nus rdo bas khyebs gyur pa'i//dbang chen rtse mo nyid du 'od bar bde gnas te;
13b6(1): mkha' lding shugs kyis nang du klu rnams dkrus pa yi//rgya mtsho rin chen gnas la rnam par gzigs pa dang //ngang pa'i tshogs can shin tu gtsang ba drag 'bab char gyi 
13b7(1): chu yis ri bag ba bkrus gyur cing //phyogs kyi glang pos bgyel ba'i sa la kyi yis rdo ba chen po mang po khyebs gyur la//gcan gzan dbang po ral pa 'khyil cing gsal 
13b8(1): bas dpung pa'i phyogs 'bar nyal bas dgar gyur cing //sbal mig 'thon pa'i lcug mas 'khor ba grub pa ngal sos ri bo kron dzar gang gshegs dang //gang dag mun   
16a1(1): @#//dri rab zhim pa'i li shi ri 'khor mang //bal glang mo rgyags drag 'gro lang long gyur//bung ba'i khyu yis me tog gzigs gyur cing //yal ga

16a8(1): bzhi'i gdan khri yod pa bsod nams su gyur pa'i sa phyogs rgyun mi 'chad par klog pa dang /kha ton dang /bsam gtan dang /ting nge 'dzin dang /snyoms par bzhugs pa dang /tshul 
16b1(1): bzhin du yid la byed pas 'jug pa dag pa'i dgra bcom pa rnams bzhugs bzhugs pa/chos gos dang /bsod snyoms dang /mal cha dang /na ba'i gso sman dang /yo 
16b2(1): byad dang /tshogs chas rnams kyi legs par byas pa/mtha' mtha' na na'i chu'i gnas yod pa la nye bar spyod pas nyams dga' ba/bde bar gshegs pa'i zhabs 
16b3(1): kyis bcas pas gtsang bar 'gyur ba'i sa gzhi can zhi ba thub pa'i skye bo gnas pa rnams na/de kho na bsgoms pas nyid kyi ting nge 'dzin bskyed pa/tshe'i lhag mas   
16b4(1): gnas par mdzad pa rnams dang /gang dag glog 'gyu bya gang dag gis sku lus bkra byas sprin snum 'dra gyur nas/bkres pa sdug bsngal snod gyur mda' rtse rnon po lta bu'i 
16b5(1): spu yis rab gang zhing /sgom pas mig zhu mi rtag bcas pa'i me rnams kyis ni kun nas lus bskor ba'i//yi dgas gnam du blta ba rnams la thugs rjes steng nas 
16b6(1): char gyi rgyun 'bebs dang //rin chen bye ma'i phreng ba gya gyu sgrigs mtho la//ngang khru bag yangs gnas shing lang long nyal gyur cing //chu dang nye bar chu'i zer mas bsil 
16b7(1): gyur pa//ma na sa'i mtsho gling bsam gtan mdzad dgu dang //gang dag ri mo mang po sha dang khrag gi ro bro ba/dga' bar gyur pa'i khra'i tshogs kyis gang bar gyur pa'i 
16b8(1): sa phyogs mgo'i thod pa gas nas//chil thigs zad pa'i snum gyis mchod pa'i mes chig cing //rang gi me lces shing mcher pa bskams shing snyis ma'i lag pa dang /
19a1(1): ci @#//tshogs kyi de kho na goms pa'i chu blugs pas mi shes pa'i sems kyi dri ma bkrus pa'i tshogs 'dus pa/nyi ma shin tu mang po nas 'dris   
19a2(1): pa'i spros pa dang bcas pa/bde bar gshegs pa'i gsung rab rin po che'i sgrom du gyur pa/dge ba'i tshogs bdud rtsi btung ba'i gzhi'o//ye shes kyi gser 
19a3(1): ma rtogs pa bdar ba'i bdar sngon por gyur pa/legs par spyad pa stong gi tshogs bsags pas grags pa rgya che ba dang ldan pa/sbyin bdag chen po bdag nyid chen po 
19a4(1): can/ston gyis nam thang ba lta bu'i yid dang ldan pa/ming 'di zhes bya ba 'di'i gan du/ma he'i rwa ltar sngo zhing dri med mkha' las 'phur//gzi brjid kyis ni nyi ma'i

19a6(1): par ting nge 'dzin dang /klog pa dang /'chag pa dang /skye bo bla ma la bsten par mngon du dga' ba'i blo gros dang ldan pa/mya ngan las 'das pa'i lam gyi rjes su    
19a7(1): 'thun pa/kha na ma mtho ba med pa'i rig pa gsum rab tu sgrub pa/nyon mongs pa las grol bar 'dod pa dge slong gang dag 'di na gnas pa/'dul ba'i cho ga la 
19a8(1): gongs pa/lus dang /ngag dang /yid kyi 'jug pa/zhi ba dang ldan pa de dag kyang ma lus par ci bder do mod kyi mtshan mo zad par mdzod la/'di nyid du gnas mdzod 
19b1(1): cig/thub pa'i sras mo shes rab zhi dang /dul bas brgyan pa'i dge slong ma/mtha' dang gling dang klu dang nags dang ri rnams spro ba'i bsam bral zhing //'dod
19b2(1): chags zhe sdang gti mug dri bral zhing 'os 'gro ba'i ma lta bu//de dag dges par mdzod la sbyin bdag phan par bzhed pas sang 'dir spyon//phyag na rdo rje 
19b3(1): la sogs sa la gnod sbyin gtso bo dang //grub pa rnams dang gang dag lha ma yin dang klu rnams dang //sangs rgyas la ni rab tu dad pa'i lha mchog gang   
19b4(1): yin pa//de dag rnams kyang sbyin bdag 'di la 'phan phyir sang spyon cig/lha dang klu dang /gnod sbyin dang /rig sngags 'chang dang grub pa dang /mi 'am 
19b5(1): ci dang /nam mkha' lding la sogs pa 'byung po chen po rnams dang /stobs bcu mnga' ba'i yon tan grub pa dang ldan pa rnams kyis kyang dam pa'i chos

19b7(1): ba'i yon tan bskyed pa las nan tan mdzod cig/rgyun mi 'chad par bsrung ba dang /bskyab pa dang /bskyang ba la brtson par mdzod cig/yang 'dul 
19b8(1): ba dang /tshul khrims dang /thos pa'i shin tu che ba'i 'phags pa'i dge slong gi dge 'dun 'dus pa la gsol ba 'tshal lo//lhun po las byung sbrul 
21a1(1): @#//dri ma dang /mi gtsang ba rnam pa sna tshogs kyis gang bar yang dag pa ji lta bzhin du so sor rtog go/dge slong dag 'di lta ste/dper 
21a2(1): na 'bras sA lu dang /'bru dang /nas dang /gro dang /mo sran gre'u dang /mo sran sde'u dang /rgya sran dang /'bras ni spa ba dang /sran chung dang /mo sran ne gu 
21a3(1): dang /til dang /yungs kar dang /'bru rnam pa sna tshogs kyis gangs ba'i sbyang zhig kha gnyis ga phye ba de la mig dang ldan pa'i mi zhig gis blugs nas so sor 
21a4(1): rtogs te/so sor rtags nas 'di dag ni 'bras sA lu'o//'di dag ni 'bru'o//'di dag ni nas so//'di dag ni gro'o//'di dag ni mon sran gre'u'o//
21a5(1): 'di dag ni mon sran sde'u'o//'di dag ni rgya sran no//'di dag ni 'bras spa ba'o//'di dag ni sran chung ngo //'di dag ni mon sran ne gu'o//'di dag ni til 
21a6(1): lo//'di dag ni 'bras drus pa'o//'di dag ni yungs kar ro zhes bya bar shes so//dge slong dag de bzhin du dgon pa na 'dug kyang rung /shing drung na 'dug 
21a7(1): kyang rung /khang stong na 'dug kyang rung ste/lus 'di nyid la 'og gi rkang mthil yan chad skra dang klad pa la thug gi bar du mi gtsang ba rnam pa sna tshogs kyis gad 
21a8(1): bar yang dag par ji lta ba bzhin du so sor rtogs ces gsungs so//dpal byin gyis zhus pa'i mdo las kyang /'di la byang chub sems dpa' lus 'di thog 
21b1(1): ma'i dang tha ma'i rgyu la so sor rtag par bya'o//pha ma'i khu chu dang khrag 'dus pa las 'byung ste/lus 'di la khu chu dang /khrag ni lus kyi thog ma'i rgyu yin no//
21b2(1): tha ma'i rgyu ni zas 'ju ba lasogs pa ste/kham gyis bza' ba zos ma thag tu bad kan gyi gnas su 'gro'o//bad kan gnas su phyin nas/bad kan gyis rkyang
